- **brainstorming**: Use this skill when the user is starting, shaping, scoping, comparing, or planning something and needs help thinking it through in conversation. Trigger on vague starts like "I want to do X," "I'm thinking about...", "help me figure this out," "which direction should I take?," or "can you brainstorm with me?" even when the domain is unspecified. Keep the work chat-first: clarify the goal, surface constraints, generate a few strong directions, narrow them, recommend a path, and summarize the current thinking.
